### B.04 Models.cs

<!-- ALTTABFLOW_FILE {"path": "Models.cs", "encoding": "utf8", "sha256": "5b1cc8848cd48b74c80851ae1bc2b87f8732293a51517afffdd61bc7f474b7fd", "bytes": 11861} -->
~~~~~csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.Win32;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Globalization;

namespace AltTabFlow;

internal sealed class Preferences
{
    public string View { get; set; } = "목록";
    public bool GroupApps { get; set; } = true;
    public bool ReplaceAltTab { get; set; } = true;
    public bool CurrentMonitorOnly { get; set; }
    public bool OnPrimaryMonitor { get; set; } = true;
    public bool ShowPreview { get; set; } = false;
    public bool LightTheme { get; set; }
    public string? ColorPreset { get; set; }
    public bool HighlightTarget { get; set; } = true;
    public bool EmphasizeTarget { get; set; } = true;
    public bool PeekTarget { get; set; } = true;
    public bool? StartWithWindows { get; set; }
    public bool AutoStartByDefault => StartWithWindows ?? true;
    public int UiScale { get; set; } = 100;
    public List<string> Pinned { get; set; } = new();
    public List<string> Excluded { get; set; } = new();
    public static string DataDirectory => Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData), "AltTabFlow");
    public static string FilePath => Path.Combine(DataDirectory, "settings.json");
    public static Preferences Load() {
        try {
            var p = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<Preferences>(File.ReadAllText(FilePath)) ?? new();
            if (p.View != "목록" && p.View != "카드" && p.View != "아이콘") p.View = "목록";
            p.UiScale = Math.Clamp(p.UiScale, 85, 140); p.Pinned ??= new(); p.Excluded ??= new();
            p.Pinned=NormalizeApps(p.Pinned);p.Excluded=NormalizeApps(p.Excluded);
            return p;
        } catch { return new(); }
    }
    internal static List<string> NormalizeApps(IEnumerable<string> values) => values.Select(x=>x.Trim()).Where(x=>x.Length>0).Select(x=>x.EndsWith(".exe",StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)?x[..^4]:x).Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();
    public void Save() {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(DataDirectory);
        var temp = FilePath + ".tmp";
        File.WriteAllText(temp, JsonSerializer.Serialize(this, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true }));
        File.Move(temp, FilePath, true);
    }
    public static bool StartupEnabled {
        get { using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(@"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run"); return key?.GetValue("AltTabFlow") != null; }
        set {
            using var key = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey(@"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run");
            if (value) key.SetValue("AltTabFlow", "\"" + Environment.ProcessPath + "\" --background");
            else key.DeleteValue("AltTabFlow", false);
        }
    }
}

internal sealed record WindowInfo(nint Handle, string Title, string ProcessName, string AppName, string Path, int Order, bool Minimized)
{
    public string GroupKey => string.IsNullOrEmpty(Path) ? ProcessName.ToLowerInvariant() : Path.ToLowerInvariant();
}

internal sealed class Entry
{
    public required List<WindowInfo> Windows { get; init; }
    public WindowInfo Window => Windows[0];
    public bool IsGroup => Windows.Count > 1;
}

internal static class WindowCatalog
{
    private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, Bitmap> Icons = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    private static readonly Bitmap FallbackIcon = SystemIcons.Application.ToBitmap();
    public static Bitmap CachedIcon(WindowInfo w) => Icons.GetValueOrDefault(w.GroupKey, FallbackIcon);
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> Names = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) {
        ["EXCEL"]="Excel", ["chrome"]="Chrome", ["msedge"]="Edge", ["explorer"]="파일 탐색기",
        ["slack"]="Slack", ["olk"]="Outlook", ["OUTLOOK"]="Outlook", ["ChatGPT"]="Codex / ChatGPT",
        ["claude"]="Claude", ["Code"]="Visual Studio Code", ["WINWORD"]="Word", ["POWERPNT"]="PowerPoint",
        ["SystemSettings"]="Windows 설정", ["notepad"]="메모장", ["p4v"]="Perforce", ["p4_toolbox"]="P4 도구모음",
        ["ApplicationFrameHost"]="Windows 앱", ["team_presence_pip"]="팀 출근 현황"
    };
    public static string AppName(string process) => Names.GetValueOrDefault(process,process);
    public static List<WindowInfo> Read() {
        var windows = new List<WindowInfo>();
        var processCache = new Dictionary<uint, (string Name, string Path)>();
        Native.EnumWindows((h, _) => {
            try {
                if (!Native.IsWindowVisible(h)) return true;
                Native.GetWindowThreadProcessId(h, out uint pid);
                if (pid == Environment.ProcessId || pid == 0) return true;
                var style = (long)Native.GetWindowLongPtr(h, -20);
                bool appWindow = (style & 0x40000) != 0;
                if (!appWindow && ((style & 0x80) != 0 || (style & 0x8000000) != 0)) return true;
                if (Native.DwmGetWindowAttribute(h, 14, out int cloaked, 4) == 0 && cloaked != 0) return true;
                string title = Native.Title(h);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title)) return true;
                string cls = Native.Class(h);
                if (cls is "Progman" or "WorkerW" or "Shell_TrayWnd" or "Shell_SecondaryTrayWnd" or "Windows.UI.Core.CoreWindow") return true;
                // Keep one visible representative for an owner/popup chain, like the native switcher.
                if (!appWindow) {
                    nint root = Native.GetAncestor(h, 3), last = root;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
                        nint popup = Native.GetLastActivePopup(last);
                        if (popup == last || popup == 0) break;
                        last = popup;
                        if (Native.IsWindowVisible(last)) break;
                    }
                    if (last != h && Native.IsWindowVisible(last)) return true;
                }
                if (!processCache.TryGetValue(pid, out var proc)) {
                    using var p = Process.GetProcessById((int)pid);
                    string path = ""; try { path = p.MainModule?.FileName ?? ""; } catch { }
                    proc = (p.ProcessName, path); processCache[pid] = proc;
                }
                string name = Names.GetValueOrDefault(proc.Name, proc.Name);
                windows.Add(new(h, title, proc.Name, name, proc.Path, windows.Count, Native.IsIconic(h)));
            } catch { /* A window may disappear while enumerating. */ }
            return true;
        }, 0);
        return windows;
    }
    public static bool Matches(WindowInfo w, string query) {
        var words = query.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
        string haystack = w.Title + " " + w.AppName + " " + w.ProcessName;
        return words.All(word => haystack.Contains(word, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
    }
    public static List<Entry> Build(IEnumerable<WindowInfo> source, Preferences p, string query, string? group, nint monitor) {
        var windows = source.Where(w => !p.Excluded.Contains(w.ProcessName, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        if (p.CurrentMonitorOnly && monitor != 0) windows = windows.Where(w => Native.MonitorFromWindow(w.Handle, 2) == monitor);
        if (group != null) windows = windows.Where(w => w.GroupKey == group);
        windows = windows.Where(w => Matches(w, query));
        List<Entry> entries;
        if (p.GroupApps && group == null && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(query))
            entries = windows.GroupBy(w => w.GroupKey).Select(g => new Entry { Windows = g.ToList() }).ToList();
        else entries = windows.Select(w => new Entry { Windows = new() { w } }).ToList();
        var pinned=p.Pinned.ToHashSet(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        return entries.OrderBy(e => pinned.Contains(e.Window.ProcessName)?0:1)
            .ThenBy(e=>e.Window.AppName,AppNameComparer.Instance)
            .ThenBy(e=>e.Window.GroupKey,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ThenBy(e => e.Window.Order).ToList();
    }
    internal static int InitialSelection(IReadOnlyList<Entry> entries,IEnumerable<WindowInfo> recency,nint origin,bool pinned,int direction){
        int current=-1;for(int i=0;i<entries.Count;i++)if(entries[i].Windows.Any(x=>x.Handle==origin)){current=i;break;}
        if(pinned||entries.Count<=1)return Math.Max(0,current);
        var candidates=recency.Select(w=>{for(int i=0;i<entries.Count;i++)if(entries[i].Windows.Any(x=>x.Handle==w.Handle))return i;return -1;}).Where(i=>i>=0&&i!=current).Distinct().ToList();
        if(candidates.Count==0)return Math.Max(0,current);
        return direction<0?candidates[^1]:candidates[0];
    }
    public static Bitmap GetIcon(WindowInfo w) {
        if (Icons.TryGetValue(w.GroupKey, out var cached)) return cached;
        Bitmap? bitmap = null;
        try {
            Native.SendMessageTimeout(w.Handle, 0x7F, 1, 0, 2, 35, out nint icon);
            if (icon == 0) icon = Native.GetClassLongPtr(w.Handle, -14);
            if (icon != 0) { using var owned = (Icon)Icon.FromHandle(icon).Clone(); bitmap = owned.ToBitmap(); }
            if (bitmap == null && File.Exists(w.Path)) { using var owned = Icon.ExtractAssociatedIcon(w.Path); bitmap = owned?.ToBitmap(); }
        } catch { }
        bitmap ??= SystemIcons.Application.ToBitmap();
        // Paint uses only this cache. Extraction runs on the catalog worker, never on Alt+Tab.
        // Keep a bounded cache for this process lifetime; do not dispose a bitmap being painted.
        if (Icons.Count < 160) Icons[w.GroupKey] = bitmap;
        else { bitmap.Dispose(); return FallbackIcon; }
        return bitmap;
    }
}

internal sealed class AppNameComparer : IComparer<string>
{
    public static readonly AppNameComparer Instance=new();
    private static readonly CompareInfo Korean=CultureInfo.GetCultureInfo("ko-KR").CompareInfo;
    private static int Kind(string name) {
        char c=name.Length>0?char.ToUpperInvariant(name[0]):'\0';
        if(c is >= 'A' and <= 'Z')return 0;
        if(c is >= '\uAC00' and <= '\uD7A3' or >= '\u1100' and <= '\u11FF' or >= '\u3130' and <= '\u318F')return 1;
        return char.IsDigit(c)?2:3;
    }
    public int Compare(string? a,string? b) {
        a=(a??"").Trim();b=(b??"").Trim();
        int category=Kind(a).CompareTo(Kind(b));
        return category!=0?category:Korean.Compare(a,b,CompareOptions.IgnoreCase|CompareOptions.IgnoreWidth);
    }
}

internal sealed class CatalogService : IDisposable
{
    private WindowInfo[] snapshot = Array.Empty<WindowInfo>();
    private readonly System.Threading.Timer timer;
    private int busy, disposed;
    public event Action? Updated;
    public WindowInfo[] Snapshot => Volatile.Read(ref snapshot);
    public double LastRefreshMs { get; private set; }
    public CatalogService() { timer = new System.Threading.Timer(_ => Refresh(), null, 0, 1800); }
    public void RefreshSoon() { if (Volatile.Read(ref disposed) == 0) try { timer.Change(60, 1800); } catch (ObjectDisposedException) { } }
    private void Refresh() {
        if (Volatile.Read(ref disposed) != 0 || Interlocked.Exchange(ref busy, 1) == 1) return;
        try {
            var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
            var rows = WindowCatalog.Read().ToArray();
            Volatile.Write(ref snapshot, rows);
            LastRefreshMs = sw.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds;
            Updated?.Invoke();
            foreach (var row in rows.GroupBy(x => x.GroupKey).Select(x => x.First())) {
                if (Volatile.Read(ref disposed) != 0) break;
                WindowCatalog.GetIcon(row);
            }
            Updated?.Invoke();
        } catch { } finally { Volatile.Write(ref busy, 0); }
    }
    public void Dispose() { Interlocked.Exchange(ref disposed, 1); timer.Dispose(); }
}
~~~~~

~~~~~csharp
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;

namespace AltTabFlow;

internal static class Native
{
    public delegate bool EnumProc(nint hwnd, nint param);
    public delegate nint HookProc(int code, nint wParam, nint lParam);
    public delegate void WinEventProc(nint hook, uint evt, nint hwnd, int objectId, int childId, uint thread, uint time);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint SetWinEventHook(uint min, uint max, nint module, WinEventProc proc, uint pid, uint tid, uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool UnhookWinEvent(nint hook);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct Rect { public int Left, Top, Right, Bottom; public Rect(Rectangle r) { Left = r.Left; Top = r.Top; Right = r.Right; Bottom = r.Bottom; } }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct Size { public int Width, Height; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct Point { public int X, Y; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct Message { public nint Hwnd; public uint Msg; public nuint WParam; public nint LParam; public uint Time; public Point Pt; public uint Private; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct Keyboard { public uint Key, ScanCode, Flags, Time; public nuint Extra; }
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct ThumbnailProperties {
        public uint Flags; public Rect Destination, Source; public byte Opacity;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] public bool Visible;
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)] public bool ClientOnly;
    }
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumProc callback, nint param);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsWindowVisible(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsWindow(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsIconic(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetWindowText(nint hwnd, StringBuilder text, int count);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern int GetClassName(nint hwnd, StringBuilder text, int count);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint="GetWindowLongPtrW")] public static extern nint GetWindowLongPtr(nint hwnd, int index);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint="GetClassLongPtrW")] public static extern nint GetClassLongPtr(nint hwnd, int index);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint GetWindow(nint hwnd, uint command);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint GetAncestor(nint hwnd, uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint GetLastActivePopup(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern uint GetWindowThreadProcessId(nint hwnd, out uint pid);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint GetForegroundWindow();
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool AllowSetForegroundWindow(uint processId);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool ShowWindowAsync(nint hwnd, int command);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(nint hwnd, int command);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool BringWindowToTop(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool AttachThreadInput(uint from, uint to, bool attach);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool IsHungAppWindow(nint hwnd);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern short GetAsyncKeyState(int key);
    [DllImport("user32.dll",SetLastError=true)] public static extern bool SetWindowPos(nint hwnd,nint after,int x,int y,int w,int h,uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint WindowFromPoint(Point point);
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential,Pack=1)] public struct BlendFunction {public byte BlendOp,BlendFlags,SourceConstantAlpha,AlphaFormat;}
    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] public struct BitmapInfo {public uint Size;public int Width,Height;public ushort Planes,BitCount;public uint Compression,SizeImage;public int XPelsPerMeter,YPelsPerMeter;public uint ClrUsed,ClrImportant;}
    [DllImport("gdi32.dll")] public static extern nint CreateCompatibleDC(nint dc);
    [DllImport("gdi32.dll")] public static extern nint CreateDIBSection(nint dc,ref BitmapInfo info,uint usage,out nint bits,nint section,uint offset);
    [DllImport("gdi32.dll")] public static extern nint SelectObject(nint dc,nint obj);
    [DllImport("gdi32.dll")] public static extern bool DeleteObject(nint obj);
    [DllImport("gdi32.dll")] public static extern bool DeleteDC(nint dc);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool UpdateLayeredWindow(nint hwnd,nint dstDc,ref Point dst,ref Size size,nint srcDc,ref Point src,uint key,ref BlendFunction blend,uint flags);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll",EntryPoint="DwmGetWindowAttribute")] public static extern int DwmGetWindowAttributeRect(nint hwnd,int attr,out Rect value,int size);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool GetWindowRect(nint hwnd, out Rect rect);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint MonitorFromWindow(nint hwnd, uint flags);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint MonitorFromPoint(Point point, uint flags);
    [DllImport("shcore.dll")] public static extern int GetDpiForMonitor(nint monitor, int type, out uint x, out uint y);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError=true)] public static extern nint SetWindowsHookEx(int id, HookProc proc, nint module, uint thread);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool UnhookWindowsHookEx(nint hook);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern nint CallNextHookEx(nint hook, int code, nint wp, nint lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern int GetMessage(out Message message, nint hwnd, uint min, uint max);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PostThreadMessage(uint id, uint msg, nuint wp, nint lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool PostMessage(nint hwnd, uint msg, nint wp, nint lp);
    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern nint SendMessageTimeout(nint hwnd, uint msg, nint wp, nint lp, uint flags, uint timeout, out nint result);
    [DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool DestroyIcon(nint icon);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode)] public static extern nint GetModuleHandle(string? module);
    [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] public static extern uint GetCurrentThreadId();
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] public static extern int DwmGetWindowAttribute(nint hwnd, int attr, out int value, int size);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] public static extern int DwmSetWindowAttribute(nint hwnd, int attr, ref int value, int size);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] public static extern int DwmRegisterThumbnail(nint dest, nint source, out nint thumbnail);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] public static extern int DwmUnregisterThumbnail(nint thumbnail);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] public static extern int DwmQueryThumbnailSourceSize(nint thumbnail, out Size size);
    [DllImport("dwmapi.dll")] public static extern int DwmUpdateThumbnailProperties(nint thumbnail, ref ThumbnailProperties properties);

    public static string Title(nint hwnd) { var s = new StringBuilder(1024); GetWindowText(hwnd, s, s.Capacity); return s.ToString(); }
    public static string Class(nint hwnd) { var s = new StringBuilder(256); GetClassName(hwnd, s, s.Capacity); return s.ToString(); }
    public static bool Activate(nint hwnd) {
        if (!IsWindow(hwnd)) return false;
        if(GetForegroundWindow()==hwnd)return true;
        var popup = GetLastActivePopup(hwnd);
        if (popup != 0 && IsWindowVisible(popup)) hwnd = popup;
        if (IsIconic(hwnd)) ShowWindowAsync(hwnd, 9);
        if(SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)) return true;
        nint foreground=GetForegroundWindow();
        if(foreground==0||IsHungAppWindow(foreground)||IsHungAppWindow(hwnd))return false;
        uint current=GetCurrentThreadId(), other=GetWindowThreadProcessId(foreground,out _);
        uint target=GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd,out _);
        bool attached=current!=other&&AttachThreadInput(current,other,true);
        bool targetAttached=target!=current&&target!=other&&AttachThreadInput(current,target,true);
        try { BringWindowToTop(hwnd); return SetForegroundWindow(hwnd); }
        finally { if(targetAttached)AttachThreadInput(current,target,false); if(attached)AttachThreadInput(current,other,false); }
    }
}
~~~~~

### B.06 KeyboardHook.cs

<!-- ALTTABFLOW_FILE {"path": "KeyboardHook.cs", "encoding": "utf8", "sha256": "0c843274e6fbfa089f8ffd7b49921829ce47e499d42724db250116b35ab67026", "bytes": 5155} -->
~~~~~csharp
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace AltTabFlow;

internal enum SwitchCommand { Next, Previous, PinnedNext, PinnedPrevious, SameApp, SameAppPrevious, Commit, Persistent, Pause, Left, Right, Up, Down }

// Pure routing: modifier values are a fresh snapshot for this shortcut, never a
// latch seeded at startup (a missed Ctrl-up used to turn Tab into a close toggle).
internal sealed class ShortcutRouter
{
    private readonly Action<SwitchCommand> post;
    private bool session, swallowedTab, swallowedGrave, swallowedSpace, swallowedPause;
    private int swallowedArrows;
    public ShortcutRouter(Action<SwitchCommand> post) { this.post=post; }
    public bool Route(uint key,bool down,bool alt,bool ctrl,bool shift,bool enabled,bool otherAltDown=false) {
        if(key is 0xA4 or 0xA5 or 0x12 && !down && session && !otherAltDown) {
            session=false;post(SwitchCommand.Commit);
        }
        if(key is >=0x25 and <=0x28) {
            int bit=1<<((int)key-0x25);
            if(session&&down){swallowedArrows|=bit;post(key==0x25?SwitchCommand.Left:key==0x26?SwitchCommand.Up:key==0x27?SwitchCommand.Right:SwitchCommand.Down);return true;}
            if(!down&&(swallowedArrows&bit)!=0){swallowedArrows&=~bit;return true;}
        }
        if(key==0x7B&&ctrl&&alt&&down){if(!swallowedPause)post(SwitchCommand.Pause);swallowedPause=true;return true;}
        if(key==0x7B&&!down&&swallowedPause){swallowedPause=false;return true;}
        if(key==0x20&&ctrl&&alt&&down){if(!swallowedSpace){session=false;post(SwitchCommand.Persistent);}swallowedSpace=true;return true;}
        if(key==0x20&&!down&&swallowedSpace){swallowedSpace=false;return true;}
        if(enabled&&alt&&down&&key==9){
            session=true;swallowedTab=true;
            post(ctrl?(shift?SwitchCommand.PinnedPrevious:SwitchCommand.PinnedNext):(shift?SwitchCommand.Previous:SwitchCommand.Next));
            return true;
        }
        if(key==9&&!down&&swallowedTab){swallowedTab=false;return true;}
        if(enabled&&alt&&!ctrl&&down&&key==0xC0){session=true;swallowedGrave=true;post(shift?SwitchCommand.SameAppPrevious:SwitchCommand.SameApp);return true;}
        if(key==0xC0&&!down&&swallowedGrave){swallowedGrave=false;return true;}
        return false;
    }
}

internal sealed class KeyboardHook : IDisposable
{
    private readonly ShortcutRouter router;
    private readonly Thread thread;
    private readonly Native.HookProc callback;
    private readonly ManualResetEventSlim ready = new();
    private nint handle;
    private uint threadId;
    private Exception? failure;
    private long eventCount, tabCount;
    private bool lastTabAlt, lastTabCtrl;
    public object Statistics => new {present=handle!=0,alive=thread.IsAlive,events=Interlocked.Read(ref eventCount),tabs=Interlocked.Read(ref tabCount),lastTabAlt,lastTabCtrl};
    public volatile bool Enabled = true;
    public KeyboardHook(Action<SwitchCommand> post) {
        router = new ShortcutRouter(post); callback = Callback;
        thread = new Thread(Run) { IsBackground = true, Name = "AltTabFlow shortcuts" };
        thread.Start(); ready.Wait(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(4));
        if (failure != null) throw failure;
        if (handle == 0) throw new InvalidOperationException("단축키 초기화에 실패했습니다.");
    }
    private void Run() {
        threadId = Native.GetCurrentThreadId();
        handle = Native.SetWindowsHookEx(13, callback, Native.GetModuleHandle(null), 0);
        if (handle == 0) failure = new Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error());
        ready.Set();
        if (handle == 0) return;
        try { while (Native.GetMessage(out _, 0, 0, 0) > 0) { } }
        finally { Native.UnhookWindowsHookEx(handle); handle = 0; }
    }
    private nint Callback(int code, nint wp, nint lp) {
        if (code < 0) return Native.CallNextHookEx(handle, code, wp, lp);
        try {
            Interlocked.Increment(ref eventCount);
            var k = Marshal.PtrToStructure<Native.Keyboard>(lp);
            bool down = (uint)wp is 0x100 or 0x104;
            bool up = (uint)wp is 0x101 or 0x105;
            if (!down && !up) return Native.CallNextHookEx(handle, code, wp, lp);
            // LLKHF_ALTDOWN belongs to this event. GetAsyncKeyState is used only
            // for OTHER keys, whose earlier transitions have already completed.
            bool alt = (k.Flags & 0x20) != 0;
            bool ctrl = Native.GetAsyncKeyState(0x11)<0, shift = Native.GetAsyncKeyState(0x10)<0;
            if(k.Key==9&&down){Interlocked.Increment(ref tabCount);lastTabAlt=alt;lastTabCtrl=ctrl;}
            bool otherAltDown=k.Key==0xA4?Native.GetAsyncKeyState(0xA5)<0:k.Key==0xA5&&Native.GetAsyncKeyState(0xA4)<0;
            if(router.Route(k.Key,down,alt,ctrl,shift,Enabled,otherAltDown))return 1;
        } catch { /* A hook must never propagate exceptions into user input. */ }
        return Native.CallNextHookEx(handle, code, wp, lp);
    }
    public void Dispose() {
        if (threadId != 0) Native.PostThreadMessage(threadId, 0x12, 0, 0);
        thread.Join(1000); ready.Dispose();
    }
}
~~~~~

### B.07 SwitcherForm.cs

<!-- ALTTABFLOW_FILE {"path": "SwitcherForm.cs", "encoding": "utf8", "sha256": "163bd0d849fec97aa6fe222a8532ec7b4ab70daf1cf5ce3caa9700fec26175da", "bytes": 32063} -->
~~~~~csharp
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace AltTabFlow;

internal sealed class SwitcherForm : Form
{
    private readonly Preferences prefs;
    private readonly CatalogService catalog;
    private readonly Func<nint,bool> activateTarget;
    private readonly TextBox search = new();
    private readonly ToolTip toolTip = new() { InitialDelay = 550, ReshowDelay = 150, AutoPopDelay = 8000 };
    private readonly List<(Rectangle Rect, Action Action, string Tip)> hits = new();
    private readonly List<nint> thumbnails = new();
    private readonly List<(WindowInfo Window, Rectangle Rect)> desiredThumbs = new();
    private string thumbnailSignature = "", lastTip = "";
    private List<WindowInfo> snapshot = new();
    private List<Entry> entries = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<nint, long> recency = new();
    private long recencyCounter;
    private int selected, page, pageSize = 6, columns = 1;
    private ListLayout listLayout=new(1,6,74,1120,750,false);
    private TileLayout tileLayout=new(3,2,1300,900);
    private string? group;
    private nint origin, monitor;
    private bool persistent, configuring, forceExit, updating, thumbnailQueued;
    private float scale = 1;
    private Rectangle content;
    private Stopwatch? opening;
    private double lastPaintMs;
    private readonly Queue<double> paintSamples = new();
    private readonly System.Windows.Forms.Timer refreshUi = new() { Interval = 100 };
    private volatile bool catalogDirty;
    private readonly Dictionary<(int Size,bool Bold),Font> fonts=new();
    private readonly Bitmap brandIcon;
    private readonly TargetAura aura=new();
    private readonly TargetPeek peek=new();
    private readonly TargetDimmer dimmer=new();
    public Action? PreferencesChanged;
    public Action<bool>? ShortcutSuppression;
    public Action? ExitRequested;
    public bool Paused { get; set; }
    public bool InspectionMode { get; set; }
    public bool IsPersistent => persistent;
    public bool IsConfiguring => configuring;
    public long InputTimestamp { get; set; }
    public string CurrentView => prefs.View;

    private ThemePalette Palette=>ThemePalette.Resolve(prefs);
    private Color Bg => Palette.Bg;
    private Color Surface => Palette.Surface;
    private Color Raised => Palette.Raised;
    private Color Ink => Palette.Ink;
    private Color Muted => Palette.Muted;
    private Color Accent => Palette.Accent;
    private Color SelectedBg => Palette.Selected;
    private int D(float v) => (int)Math.Round(v * scale);
    private Rectangle R(float x, float y, float w, float h) => new(D(x), D(y), D(w), D(h));
    private float W => ClientSize.Width / scale;
    private float H => ClientSize.Height / scale;

    public SwitcherForm(Preferences prefs, CatalogService catalog,Func<nint,bool>? activateTarget=null) {
        this.prefs = prefs; this.catalog = catalog;
        this.activateTarget=activateTarget??Native.Activate;
        using(var stream=typeof(SwitcherForm).Assembly.GetManifestResourceStream("AltTabFlow.Assets.AltTabFlow.ico"))
        using(var icon=new Icon(stream!,48,48)) { brandIcon=icon.ToBitmap(); Icon=(Icon)icon.Clone(); }
        Text = "AltTabFlow · 창 전환"; Name = "AltTabFlow";
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.None; StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
        ShowInTaskbar = false; TopMost = true; KeyPreview = true; AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.None;
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint | ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
        search.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
        search.PlaceholderText = "창 제목이나 앱 이름으로 검색";
        search.AccessibleName = "창 제목 또는 앱 이름 검색";
        search.TextChanged += (_, _) => { if (!updating) { persistent=true; selected = page = 0; Rebuild(false); } };
        search.MouseDown += (_, _) => { persistent = true; Invalidate(); };
        Controls.Add(search);
        Deactivate += (_, _) => { if (Visible && !configuring && !InspectionMode) Dismiss(false); };
        MouseWheel += (_, e) => { persistent=true; if (entries.Count > 0) MoveSelection(e.Delta > 0 ? -1 : 1); };
        refreshUi.Tick += (_, _) => {
            if (!Visible || configuring || !catalogDirty) return;
            catalogDirty = false;
            // Refresh membership/title without reordering a selection while the user navigates.
            var fresh = catalog.Snapshot;
            var keep = snapshot.Where(w => fresh.Any(f => f.Handle == w.Handle)).Select(w => fresh.First(f => f.Handle == w.Handle) with {Order=w.Order}).ToList();
            keep.AddRange(fresh.Where(w => keep.All(k => k.Handle != w.Handle)));
            snapshot = keep.Select((w,i)=>w with {Order=i}).ToList(); Rebuild(true);
        };
        catalog.Updated += OnCatalogUpdated;
    }
    private void OnCatalogUpdated() { catalogDirty = true; }
    protected override CreateParams CreateParams { get { var p = base.CreateParams; if(!InspectionMode)p.ExStyle |= 0x80; return p; } }
    protected override void OnHandleCreated(EventArgs e) {
        base.OnHandleCreated(e);
        int rounded = 2; Native.DwmSetWindowAttribute(Handle, 33, ref rounded, 4);
    }
    protected override void WndProc(ref Message m) {
        // Releasing Alt must not enter the invisible native system menu.
        if(m.Msg==0x112&&((long)m.WParam&0xFFF0)==0xF100)return;
        base.WndProc(ref m);
    }
    public void TrackForeground(nint hwnd) {
        if (hwnd == 0 || hwnd == Handle) return;
        Native.GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, out uint pid);
        if (pid == Environment.ProcessId) return;
        recency[hwnd] = ++recencyCounter;
        if (recency.Count > 512) { var alive = catalog.Snapshot.Select(x => x.Handle).ToHashSet(); foreach (var h in recency.Keys.Where(x => !alive.Contains(x)).ToArray()) recency.Remove(h); }
        if (!catalog.Snapshot.Any(w => w.Handle == hwnd)) catalog.RefreshSoon();
    }
    public void OpenSwitcher(bool pinned, int direction = 1, bool sameApp = false) {
        if (Visible) { persistent |= pinned; Activate(); Native.SetForegroundWindow(Handle); MoveSelection(direction); return; }
        opening = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        origin = Native.GetForegroundWindow(); monitor = Native.MonitorFromWindow(origin, 2);
        TrackForeground(origin);
        snapshot = catalog.Snapshot.OrderByDescending(w => recency.GetValueOrDefault(w.Handle)).ThenBy(w => w.Order).Select((w,i)=>w with {Order=i}).ToList();
        group = sameApp ? snapshot.FirstOrDefault(w => w.Handle == origin)?.GroupKey : null;
        persistent = pinned;
        updating = true; search.Clear(); updating = false;
        selected = page = 0;
        Rebuild(false);
        ApplyBounds();
        selected=WindowCatalog.InitialSelection(entries,snapshot,origin,pinned,direction);
        EnsurePage(); ApplyTheme();
        Show(); Native.ShowWindow(Handle,5); Activate(); Native.Activate(Handle);
        if (pinned) search.Focus(); else ActiveControl = null;
        refreshUi.Start();
        Invalidate();
        // Enumeration and icon extraction stay off the shortcut path.
        catalog.RefreshSoon();
    }
    private void ApplyBounds() {
        var screen=prefs.OnPrimaryMonitor?(Screen.PrimaryScreen??Screen.FromHandle(origin)):Screen.FromHandle(origin);
        var screenMonitor=Native.MonitorFromPoint(new Native.Point{X=screen.Bounds.Left+1,Y=screen.Bounds.Top+1},2);
        uint dpi=(uint)DeviceDpi;
        if(Native.GetDpiForMonitor(screenMonitor,0,out uint monitorDpi,out _)==0)dpi=monitorDpi;
        scale = Math.Clamp(dpi / 96f * prefs.UiScale / 100f, .8f, 3f);
        var work = screen.WorkingArea;
        listLayout=LayoutPlanner.Plan(entries.Count,(work.Width-48)/scale,(work.Height-64)/scale,prefs.ShowPreview);
        tileLayout=LayoutPlanner.PlanTiles(entries.Count,(work.Width-48)/scale,(work.Height-64)/scale,prefs.View=="아이콘");
        int width=prefs.View=="목록"?D(listLayout.Width):D(tileLayout.Width);
        int height=prefs.View=="목록"?D(listLayout.Height):D(tileLayout.Height);
        if (width / scale < 900 || height / scale < 580) scale = Math.Min(width / 900f, height / 580f);
        Bounds = new Rectangle(work.Left + (work.Width-width)/2, work.Top+(work.Height-height)/2, width, height);
        search.Bounds = R(64, 92, W - 142, 29);
        search.Font=F(15);
        RecalculatePageSize();
    }
    private void ApplyTheme() { BackColor = Bg; search.BackColor = Surface; search.ForeColor = Ink; int dark = Palette.Light ? 0 : 1; Native.DwmSetWindowAttribute(Handle, 20, ref dark, 4); }
    private static int Mod(int x, int n) => n == 0 ? 0 : (x % n + n) % n;
    private void RecalculatePageSize() {
        content = R(28, 229, W - 56, H - 300);
        if (prefs.View == "목록") { columns=1;pageSize=listLayout.Capacity; }
        else {columns=tileLayout.Columns;pageSize=tileLayout.Capacity;}
    }
    private void Rebuild(bool keep) {
        int beforeCount=entries.Count;
        nint prev = keep && selected < entries.Count ? entries[selected].Window.Handle : 0;
        entries = WindowCatalog.Build(snapshot, prefs, search.Text, group, monitor);
        if (prev != 0) { int index = entries.FindIndex(e => e.Windows.Any(w => w.Handle == prev)); if (index >= 0) selected = index; }
        if(Visible&&beforeCount!=entries.Count&&search.TextLength==0)ApplyBounds();
        selected = Math.Clamp(selected, 0, Math.Max(0, entries.Count-1)); EnsurePage();
        UpdateAura();
        Invalidate();
    }
    private void EnsurePage() { page = selected / Math.Max(1, pageSize); }
    public void MoveSelection(int offset) { if (entries.Count == 0) return; selected = Mod(selected+offset, entries.Count); EnsurePage(); UpdateAura(); Invalidate(); }
    private void UpdateAura(){
        if(!Visible||configuring||selected>=entries.Count){HideTargetVisuals();return;}
        nint hwnd=entries[selected].Window.Handle;
        if(prefs.PeekTarget)peek.Select(hwnd,Handle);else peek.Restore();
        if(prefs.EmphasizeTarget)dimmer.Select(hwnd,Handle);else dimmer.Hide();
        if(prefs.HighlightTarget)aura.Select(hwnd,Handle,Accent,prefs.EmphasizeTarget);else aura.Hide();
    }
    private void HideTargetVisuals(){aura.Hide();dimmer.Hide();peek.Restore();}
    public void AltReleased() { if (Visible && !persistent && !configuring) Choose(); }
    public void TogglePersistent() { if (Visible) Dismiss(true); else OpenSwitcher(true, 0); }
    public void Dismiss(bool restore) {
        refreshUi.Stop(); opening=null; HideTargetVisuals(); ClearThumbnails(); Hide(); persistent=false;
        if (restore && Native.IsWindow(origin)) Native.Activate(origin);
    }
    private void Choose() {
        if (selected >= entries.Count) { Dismiss(true); return; }
        var target = entries[selected].Window;
        peek.Accept();
        HideTargetVisuals();
        TrackForeground(target.Handle);
        bool success=activateTarget(target.Handle);
        Dismiss(false);
        AppLog.Write(success?"activated":"activate-failed",new {hwnd=target.Handle.ToInt64(),foreground=Native.GetForegroundWindow().ToInt64(),inputMs=InputTimestamp>0?Math.Round(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(InputTimestamp).TotalMilliseconds,2):0});
        InputTimestamp=0;
    }
    private void ExpandGroup() {
        if (selected >= entries.Count || !entries[selected].IsGroup) return;
        persistent=true;
        group = entries[selected].Window.GroupKey; selected=page=0;
        updating=true; search.Clear(); updating=false; Rebuild(false);
    }
    private void GoBack() {
        string? previous=group; group=null; selected=page=0; Rebuild(false);
        int index=entries.FindIndex(x=>x.Window.GroupKey==previous); if(index>=0){selected=index;EnsurePage();UpdateAura();Invalidate();}
    }
    private void SetView(string mode) { persistent=true; prefs.View=mode; SavePreferences(); ClearThumbnails(); ApplyBounds(); EnsurePage(); Invalidate(); }
    public void DetailedNavigate(Keys key) {
        if(!Visible||configuring)return;
        NavigateArrow(key);
    }
    private void NavigateArrow(Keys key) {
        persistent=true;
        int delta;
        if(prefs.View=="목록")delta=key==Keys.Up?-1:key==Keys.Down?1:key==Keys.Left?-(listLayout.Columns>1?listLayout.Rows:1):(listLayout.Columns>1?listLayout.Rows:1);
        else delta=key==Keys.Up?-columns:key==Keys.Down?columns:key==Keys.Left?-1:1;
        MoveSelection(delta);Invalidate();
    }
    private void SavePreferences() { try { prefs.Save(); PreferencesChanged?.Invoke(); } catch (Exception ex) { AppLog.Write("settings-save-failed", new {ex.Message}); } }
    protected override bool ProcessCmdKey(ref Message msg, Keys keyData) {
        Keys key = keyData & Keys.KeyCode; bool ctrl=(keyData & Keys.Control)!=0, shift=(keyData & Keys.Shift)!=0;
        if (key == Keys.Escape) {
            Dismiss(true); return true;
        }
        if (key == Keys.Enter) { if (selected<entries.Count&&entries[selected].IsGroup) ExpandGroup(); else Choose(); return true; }
        if (key == Keys.Back && search.TextLength==0 && !ctrl) { if(group!=null){persistent=true;GoBack();} return true; }
        if (key == Keys.Home && search.TextLength==0) { persistent=true;selected=0;EnsurePage();UpdateAura();Invalidate();return true; }
        if (key == Keys.End && search.TextLength==0) { persistent=true;selected=Math.Max(0,entries.Count-1);EnsurePage();UpdateAura();Invalidate();return true; }
        if (key == Keys.Tab) { MoveSelection(shift ? -1 : 1); return true; }
        if(key is Keys.Up or Keys.Down or Keys.Left or Keys.Right) {
            persistent=true;
            if((key is Keys.Left or Keys.Right)&&search.Focused&&search.TextLength>0){Invalidate();return base.ProcessCmdKey(ref msg,keyData);}
            NavigateArrow(key);return true;
        }
        if (key == Keys.PageDown) { persistent=true;MoveSelection(pageSize); return true; }
        if (key == Keys.PageUp) { persistent=true;MoveSelection(-pageSize); return true; }
        if (ctrl && key is Keys.D1 or Keys.D2 or Keys.D3) { SetView(key == Keys.D1 ? "목록" : key == Keys.D2 ? "카드" : "아이콘"); return true; }
        if (ctrl && key == Keys.F) { persistent=true; search.Focus(); search.SelectAll(); Invalidate(); return true; }
        if (ctrl && key == Keys.P) { TogglePin(); return true; }
        if (key == Keys.F5) { catalog.RefreshSoon(); return true; }
        if (key == Keys.F2) { ShowSettings(); return true; }
        // Printable input is routed to a real edit control so the Korean IME works normally.
        if (!search.Focused && (keyData & Keys.Alt) == 0 && !ctrl && key is >= Keys.D0 and <= Keys.Z) { persistent=true; search.Focus(); }
        return base.ProcessCmdKey(ref msg, keyData);
    }
    protected override void OnFormClosing(FormClosingEventArgs e) { if (!forceExit) { e.Cancel=true; Dismiss(true); } base.OnFormClosing(e); }
    public void Shutdown() { forceExit=true; catalog.Updated-=OnCatalogUpdated; HideTargetVisuals(); ClearThumbnails(); refreshUi.Dispose(); toolTip.Dispose(); Close(); aura.Dispose();dimmer.Dispose();peek.Dispose(); foreach(var font in fonts.Values)font.Dispose(); fonts.Clear(); brandIcon.Dispose(); }
    private void TogglePin() {
        if (selected >= entries.Count) return; string key=entries[selected].Window.ProcessName;
        int index=prefs.Pinned.FindIndex(x=>x.Equals(key,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)); if(index>=0)prefs.Pinned.RemoveAt(index);else prefs.Pinned.Add(key); SavePreferences(); Rebuild(true);
    }
    private void ShowItemMenu(int index, Point point) {
        selected=index; Invalidate(); persistent=true;
        var item=entries[index]; var menu=new ContextMenuStrip();
        menu.Items.Add("이 창으로 전환", null, (_,_)=>Choose());
        if(item.IsGroup) menu.Items.Add($"{item.Windows.Count}개 창 펼치기",null,(_,_)=>ExpandGroup());
        menu.Items.Add(prefs.Pinned.Contains(item.Window.ProcessName,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? "상단 정렬 해제" : "상단 정렬에 등록",null,(_,_)=>TogglePin());
        menu.Items.Add("이 앱을 목록에서 숨기기",null,(_,_)=> { prefs.Excluded.Add(item.Window.ProcessName); SavePreferences(); Rebuild(false); });
        menu.Closed += (_,_)=> { configuring=false; ShortcutSuppression?.Invoke(false); menu.Dispose(); };
        configuring=true; ShortcutSuppression?.Invoke(true); menu.Show(this, point);
    }
    protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e) {
        base.OnMouseDown(e);
        if(e.Button==MouseButtons.Right) {
            int i=HitEntry(e.Location); if(i>=0) ShowItemMenu(i,e.Location); return;
        }
        if(e.Button!=MouseButtons.Left) return;
        foreach(var hit in hits.AsEnumerable().Reverse()) if(hit.Rect.Contains(e.Location)) { hit.Action(); return; }
    }
    private readonly Dictionary<int,Rectangle> entryRects=new();
    private int HitEntry(Point p) { foreach(var kv in entryRects) if(kv.Value.Contains(p)) return kv.Key; return -1; }
    protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e) {
        base.OnMouseMove(e); string tip="";
        foreach(var hit in hits.AsEnumerable().Reverse()) if(hit.Rect.Contains(e.Location)) { tip=hit.Tip; break; }
        Cursor=hits.Any(h=>h.Rect.Contains(e.Location)) ? Cursors.Hand : Cursors.Default;
        if(tip!=lastTip) { toolTip.SetToolTip(this, tip); lastTip=tip; }
    }
    private Font F(float size,bool bold=false) {
        var key=(D(size),bold);
        if(!fonts.TryGetValue(key,out var font)){font=new Font("맑은 고딕",key.Item1,bold?FontStyle.Bold:FontStyle.Regular,GraphicsUnit.Pixel);fonts[key]=font;}
        return font;
    }
    private void Txt(Graphics g,string text,Rectangle rect,float size,Color color,bool bold=false,bool wrap=false,TextFormatFlags extra=0) {
        var font=F(size,bold);
        TextRenderer.DrawText(g,text,font,rect,color,TextFormatFlags.NoPrefix | TextFormatFlags.NoPadding | (wrap?TextFormatFlags.WordBreak:TextFormatFlags.SingleLine|TextFormatFlags.EndEllipsis) | extra);
    }
    private static GraphicsPath RoundPath(Rectangle r,int radius) {
        var p=new GraphicsPath(); int d=Math.Min(radius*2,Math.Min(r.Width,r.Height));
        if(d<=0) { p.AddRectangle(r); return p; }
        p.AddArc(r.X,r.Y,d,d,180,90); p.AddArc(r.Right-d,r.Y,d,d,270,90); p.AddArc(r.Right-d,r.Bottom-d,d,d,0,90); p.AddArc(r.X,r.Bottom-d,d,d,90,90); p.CloseFigure(); return p;
    }
    private void Box(Graphics g,Rectangle r,Color fill,int radius=12,Color? stroke=null) {
        using var path=RoundPath(r,D(radius)); using var brush=new SolidBrush(fill); g.FillPath(brush,path);
        if(stroke!=null) { using var pen=new Pen(stroke.Value,D(1)); g.DrawPath(pen,path); }
    }
    private void Button(Graphics g,string text,Rectangle rect,Action action,bool active=false,string tip="") {
        Box(g,rect,active?SelectedBg:Raised,8); Txt(g,text,Rectangle.Inflate(rect,-D(8),0),13,active?Accent:Ink,active,false,TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter|TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
        hits.Add((rect,action,tip));
    }
    protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e) {
        var g=e.Graphics; g.SmoothingMode=SmoothingMode.AntiAlias; g.Clear(Bg);
        hits.Clear(); entryRects.Clear(); desiredThumbs.Clear();
        g.DrawImage(brandIcon,R(23,20,46,46));
        Txt(g,"AltTabFlow",R(74,22,300,30),23,Ink,true);
        Txt(g,persistent?"상세 선택 · Enter 전환 / Esc 닫기":"빠른 전환 · Alt를 놓으면 전환",R(75,54,470,22),12,persistent?Accent:Muted);
        Button(g,"설정",R(W-158,31,66,32),ShowSettings,false,"F2 · 보기, 단축키, 제외 목록");
        Button(g,"닫기",R(W-82,31,54,32),()=>Dismiss(true),false,"Esc · 원래 창으로 돌아가기");
        Box(g,R(28,84,W-56,47),Surface,12);
        using(var pen=new Pen(Muted,D(1.8f))) { g.DrawEllipse(pen,D(42),D(99),D(11),D(11)); g.DrawLine(pen,D(51),D(108),D(56),D(113)); }
        if(search.Text.Length>0) Button(g,"×",R(W-65,92,28,28),()=>search.Clear());
        string[] modes={"목록","카드","아이콘"};
        for(int i=0;i<modes.Length;i++) { string mode=modes[i]; Button(g,mode,R(28+i*75,147,67,34),()=>SetView(mode),prefs.View==mode,$"Ctrl+{i+1}"); }
        Button(g,prefs.GroupApps?"앱별 묶기  켜짐":"앱별 묶기  꺼짐",R(272,147,142,34),()=> { persistent=true;prefs.GroupApps=!prefs.GroupApps; group=null; SavePreferences(); Rebuild(false); },prefs.GroupApps);
        if(prefs.View=="목록")Button(g,"미리보기",R(429,147,89,34),()=> {persistent=true;prefs.ShowPreview=!prefs.ShowPreview;SavePreferences();ClearThumbnails();ApplyBounds();EnsurePage();Invalidate();},listLayout.Preview,"목록 옆에 미리보기 표시 · 화면이 좁으면 자동 접힘");
        int total=entries.Sum(x=>x.Windows.Count);
        Txt(g,$"{entries.Count}개 항목  ·  {total}개 창",R(W-275,155,247,24),13,Muted,false,false,TextFormatFlags.Right);
        if(group!=null) {
            Button(g,"‹ 전체 앱",R(28,194,95,26),GoBack,false,"← · 앱 목록으로 돌아가기");
            Txt(g,snapshot.FirstOrDefault(w=>w.GroupKey==group)?.AppName??"앱의 창",R(137,197,W-300,24),13,Accent,true);
        } else Txt(g,search.Text.Length>0?"검색 결과 · 제목을 확인하고 바로 전환하세요":prefs.Pinned.Count>0?"상단 등록 앱 먼저 · 각 그룹 A~Z → 가나다순":"앱 이름순 · A~Z → 가나다 · Enter로 묶음 펼치기",R(28,199,W-56,24),12,Muted);
        if(entries.Count==0) {
            Txt(g,catalog.Snapshot.Length==0?"열린 창을 준비하고 있습니다":"일치하는 창이 없습니다",R(60,320,W-120,40),22,Ink,true,false,TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
            Txt(g,"검색어를 줄이거나 설정에서 숨긴 앱을 확인해 주세요.",R(60,367,W-120,30),14,Muted,false,false,TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
        } else if(prefs.View=="목록") PaintList(g); else PaintTiles(g);
        using(var pen=new Pen(Raised)) g.DrawLine(pen,D(28),D(H-61),D(W-28),D(H-61));
        Txt(g,persistent?"방향키 선택   Enter 펼치기 / 전환   Backspace 뒤로   Esc 닫기":"Tab 다음   Enter 묶음 펼치기   Alt를 놓으면 전환   Ctrl+F 검색",R(28,H-43,W-180,28),12,Muted);
        int pages=Math.Max(1,(entries.Count+pageSize-1)/pageSize);
        if(pages>1){Button(g,"‹",R(W-149,H-47,29,29),()=>MovePage(-1),false,"이전 페이지");Txt(g,$"{page+1} / {pages}",R(W-115,H-42,52,25),12,Muted,false,false,TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);Button(g,"›",R(W-57,H-47,29,29),()=>MovePage(1),false,"다음 페이지");}
        else Txt(g,"전체 목록",R(W-144,H-42,116,25),12,Accent,false,false,TextFormatFlags.Right);
        QueueThumbnails();
        if(opening!=null) {
            lastPaintMs=opening.Elapsed.TotalMilliseconds; paintSamples.Enqueue(lastPaintMs); if(paintSamples.Count>100) paintSamples.Dequeue(); opening=null;
            AppLog.Write("first-paint",new{milliseconds=Math.Round(lastPaintMs,2),keyToPaintMs=InputTimestamp>0?Math.Round(Stopwatch.GetElapsedTime(InputTimestamp).TotalMilliseconds,2):0,windows=snapshot.Count,view=prefs.View});
            // Only schedule the effect after the chooser has actually painted.
            BeginInvoke((Action)(()=>{if(Visible)UpdateAura();}));
        }
        base.OnPaint(e);
    }
    private void MovePage(int delta) { if(entries.Count==0) return; int pages=(entries.Count+pageSize-1)/pageSize; selected=Mod(page+delta,pages)*pageSize; EnsurePage(); UpdateAura(); Invalidate(); }
    private void PaintList(Graphics g) {
        float listWidth=listLayout.Preview?W-56-400:W-56;
        float width=(listWidth-(listLayout.Columns-1)*14)/listLayout.Columns;
        int start=page*pageSize;
        for(int i=start;i<Math.Min(entries.Count,start+pageSize);i++) {
            int index=i,local=i-start;var item=entries[i];var row=R(28+(local/listLayout.Rows)*(width+14),229+(local%listLayout.Rows)*listLayout.Pitch,width,listLayout.Pitch-8);
            bool active=i==selected;
            Box(g,row,active?SelectedBg:Surface,10,active?Accent:null);
            var icon=WindowCatalog.CachedIcon(item.Window);
            g.DrawImage(icon,new Rectangle(row.X+D(15),row.Y+(row.Height-D(32))/2,D(32),D(32)));
            bool pin=prefs.Pinned.Contains(item.Window.ProcessName,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            Txt(g,(pin?"★  ":"")+item.Window.AppName,new Rectangle(row.X+D(61),row.Y+D(7),row.Width-D(item.IsGroup?150:80),D(23)),14,active?Accent:Ink,true);
            Txt(g,(item.Window.Minimized?"최소화 · 선택 시 복원 | ":"")+item.Window.Title,new Rectangle(row.X+D(61),row.Y+D(listLayout.Pitch<70?30:35),row.Width-D(76),D(22)),13,Muted);
            entryRects[i]=row;
            hits.Add((row,()=> { selected=index; Choose(); },item.Window.Title));
            if(item.IsGroup) Button(g,$"{item.Windows.Count}개  ›",new Rectangle(row.Right-D(81),row.Y+D(6),D(66),D(25)),()=> { selected=index; ExpandGroup(); },active,"앱의 모든 창 제목 보기");
        }
        if(!listLayout.Preview||selected>=entries.Count) return;
        var selectedEntry=entries[selected]; var w=selectedEntry.Window;
        float px=28+listWidth+20, pw=W-28-px, py=229;
        Box(g,R(px,py,pw,H-300),Surface,12);
        var preview=R(px+16,py+16,pw-32,Math.Min(247,H-456));
        PaintPreview(g,w,preview);
        float ty=py+16+preview.Height/scale+20;
        Txt(g,w.AppName,R(px+20,ty,pw-40,25),16,Ink,true);
        Txt(g,w.Title,R(px+20,ty+35,pw-40,63),14,Muted,false,true);
        string tag=w.Minimized?"최소화된 창 · 선택하면 복원":"선택한 창 미리보기";
        Txt(g,tag,R(px+20,H-160,pw-40,23),11,Muted);
        Button(g,"이 창으로 전환  ↵",R(px+20,H-125,pw-40,36),Choose,true);
    }
    private void PaintTiles(Graphics g) {
        int start=page*pageSize; float gap=12, tw=(W-56-(columns-1)*gap)/columns;
        float th=prefs.View=="아이콘"?146:204;
        for(int i=start;i<Math.Min(entries.Count,start+pageSize);i++) {
            int index=i, local=i-start; var item=entries[i]; float x=28+(local%columns)*(tw+gap), y=229+(local/columns)*(th+12);
            var rect=R(x,y,tw,th); bool active=i==selected;
            Box(g,rect,active?SelectedBg:Surface,12,active?Accent:null);
            if(prefs.View=="카드") {
                PaintPreview(g,item.Window,R(x+12,y+12,tw-24,118));
                g.DrawImage(WindowCatalog.CachedIcon(item.Window),R(x+14,y+144,24,24));
                Txt(g,item.Window.AppName,R(x+46,y+145,tw-105,23),13,Ink,true);
                Txt(g,item.Window.Title,R(x+14,y+177,tw-28,22),12,Muted);
            } else {
                g.DrawImage(WindowCatalog.CachedIcon(item.Window),R(x+(tw-40)/2,y+18,40,40));
                Txt(g,item.Window.AppName,R(x+10,y+72,tw-20,24),13,Ink,true,false,TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
                Txt(g,item.Window.Title,R(x+12,y+104,tw-24,24),11,Muted,false,false,TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
            }
            entryRects[i]=rect; hits.Add((rect,()=> { selected=index; Choose(); },item.Window.Title));
            if(item.IsGroup) Button(g,$"{item.Windows.Count} ›",R(x+tw-56,y+6,48,27),()=> { selected=index; ExpandGroup(); },active,"모든 창 제목 펼치기");
        }
    }
    private void PaintPreview(Graphics g,WindowInfo w,Rectangle rect) {
        Box(g,rect,Raised,8);
        var icon=WindowCatalog.CachedIcon(w);
        g.DrawImage(icon,new Rectangle(rect.X+(rect.Width-D(38))/2,rect.Y+(rect.Height-D(50))/2,D(38),D(38)));
        Txt(g,w.Minimized?"최소화된 창":"미리보기",new Rectangle(rect.X,rect.Bottom-D(25),rect.Width,D(20)),11,Muted,false,false,TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter);
        if(!w.Minimized) desiredThumbs.Add((w,rect));
    }
    private void QueueThumbnails() {
        if(thumbnailQueued||!Visible) return;
        string signature=string.Join(";",desiredThumbs.Select(t=>$"{t.Window.Handle}:{t.Rect}"));
        if(signature==thumbnailSignature) return;
        thumbnailQueued=true;
        BeginInvoke((Action)(()=> {
            thumbnailQueued=false; ClearThumbnails(false);
            if(!Visible) return;
            var wanted=desiredThumbs.ToArray();
            thumbnailSignature=string.Join(";",wanted.Select(t=>$"{t.Window.Handle}:{t.Rect}"));
            foreach(var t in wanted) {
                if(Native.DwmRegisterThumbnail(Handle,t.Window.Handle,out nint thumb)!=0) continue;
                thumbnails.Add(thumb);
                if(Native.DwmQueryThumbnailSourceSize(thumb,out var size)!=0||size.Width<=0||size.Height<=0) continue;
                float fit=Math.Min((float)t.Rect.Width/size.Width,(float)t.Rect.Height/size.Height);
                int w=(int)(size.Width*fit),h=(int)(size.Height*fit);
                var dest=new Rectangle(t.Rect.X+(t.Rect.Width-w)/2,t.Rect.Y+(t.Rect.Height-h)/2,w,h);
                var p=new Native.ThumbnailProperties { Flags=0x1|0x4|0x8|0x10,Destination=new Native.Rect(dest),Opacity=255,Visible=true,ClientOnly=false };
                Native.DwmUpdateThumbnailProperties(thumb,ref p);
                if(InspectionMode) AppLog.Write("thumbnail",new{dest=dest.ToString(),sourceSize=$"{size.Width}x{size.Height}",structSize=System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.SizeOf<Native.ThumbnailProperties>(),bounds=Bounds.ToString(),dpi=DeviceDpi});
            }
        }));
    }
    private void ClearThumbnails(bool reset=true) { foreach(var h in thumbnails) Native.DwmUnregisterThumbnail(h); thumbnails.Clear(); if(reset)thumbnailSignature=""; }
    public void ShowSettings() {
        persistent=true; configuring=true; HideTargetVisuals(); ShortcutSuppression?.Invoke(true); ClearThumbnails();
        try {
            using var dialog=new SettingsForm(prefs,catalog.Snapshot);
            if(dialog.ShowDialog(this)==DialogResult.OK) { SavePreferences(); ApplyBounds(); ApplyTheme(); group=null; Rebuild(true); }
        } finally { configuring=false; ShortcutSuppression?.Invoke(false); if(Visible) { Activate(); UpdateAura(); Invalidate(); } }
    }
    public object DiagnosticState() => new {
        visible=Visible, nativeVisible=Native.IsWindowVisible(Handle), ownHwnd=Handle.ToInt64(), foreground=Native.GetForegroundWindow().ToInt64(), prefs.View, prefs.GroupApps, persistent, selected, page, pageSize,
        query=search.Text, group, selectedHwnd=selected<entries.Count?entries[selected].Window.Handle.ToInt64():0,
        totalWindows=snapshot.Count, totalEntries=entries.Count, thumbnails=thumbnails.Count,
        lastPaintMs, samples=paintSamples.ToArray(), refreshMs=catalog.LastRefreshMs,
        colorPreset=Palette.Id,prefs.StartWithWindows,prefs.HighlightTarget,prefs.EmphasizeTarget,prefs.PeekTarget,aura=aura.Statistics,peek=peek.Statistics,
        bounds=Bounds.ToString(),scale,listColumns=listLayout.Columns,listRows=listLayout.Rows,allItemsVisible=entries.Count<=pageSize,previewRects=desiredThumbs.Select(x=>x.Rect.ToString()).ToArray(),
        prefs.OnPrimaryMonitor,display=Screen.FromControl(this).DeviceName,onPrimary=Screen.FromControl(this).Primary,primaryBounds=Screen.PrimaryScreen?.Bounds.ToString(),
        entries=entries.Select(x=>new {app=x.Window.AppName,title=x.Window.Title,count=x.Windows.Count,hwnd=x.Window.Handle.ToInt64()}).ToArray()
    };
    // Exercise the real keyboard routing on an off-screen form without moving user windows.
    internal void PrepareNavigationTest(List<WindowInfo> windows){snapshot=windows;selected=page=0;group=null;persistent=false;Rebuild(false);ApplyBounds();}
    internal bool TestKey(Keys key){var message=Message.Create(Handle,0x100,(nint)(int)key,0);return ProcessCmdKey(ref message,key);}
    internal (string? Group,int Count,bool Persistent,nint Selected) NavigationState=>(group,entries.Count,persistent,selected<entries.Count?entries[selected].Window.Handle:0);
}
~~~~~

### B.08 LayoutPlanner.cs

<!-- ALTTABFLOW_FILE {"path": "LayoutPlanner.cs", "encoding": "utf8", "sha256": "34db34e6995d66f79e97ebe0ca740fca3bcaf66439d59d488218e9eed930f934", "bytes": 2303} -->
~~~~~csharp
namespace AltTabFlow;

internal sealed record ListLayout(int Columns,int Rows,int Pitch,float Width,float Height,bool Preview)
{
    public int Capacity=>Columns*Rows;
}
internal sealed record TileLayout(int Columns,int Rows,float Width,float Height){public int Capacity=>Columns*Rows;}

internal static class LayoutPlanner
{
    public static TileLayout PlanTiles(int count,float availableWidth,float availableHeight,bool icons) {
        count=Math.Max(1,count);int pitch=icons?158:216;
        int maxCols=Math.Max(1,(int)((availableWidth-44)/(icons?162:272)));
        int maxRows=Math.Max(1,(int)((Math.Min(1400,availableHeight)-300)/pitch));
        if(count>maxCols*maxRows)maxRows=Math.Max(1,(int)((availableHeight-300)/pitch));
        int preferred=icons?5:Math.Max(3,(int)Math.Ceiling(Math.Sqrt(count)));
        int cols=Math.Clamp(Math.Max(preferred,(count+maxRows-1)/maxRows),1,maxCols);
        int rows=Math.Min(maxRows,(count+cols-1)/cols);
        float width=Math.Min(availableWidth,Math.Max(900,56+cols*(icons?190:300)+(cols-1)*12));
        float height=Math.Min(availableHeight,Math.Max(590,300+rows*pitch));
        return new(cols,rows,width,height);
    }
    public static ListLayout Plan(int count,float availableWidth,float availableHeight,bool preview) {
        count=Math.Max(1,count);
        float maxWidth=Math.Max(500,availableWidth),maxHeight=Math.Max(450,availableHeight);
        int pitch=74;
        int maxColumns=Math.Max(1,(int)((maxWidth-56)/330));
        int maxRows=Math.Max(1,(int)((Math.Min(1100,maxHeight)-300)/pitch));
        if(count>maxColumns*maxRows)maxRows=Math.Max(1,(int)((maxHeight-300)/pitch));
        if(count>maxColumns*maxRows) {pitch=62;maxRows=Math.Max(1,(int)((maxHeight-300)/pitch));}
        if(count>maxColumns*maxRows)maxColumns=Math.Max(1,(int)((maxWidth-56)/250));
        int columns=Math.Clamp((count+maxRows-1)/maxRows,1,maxColumns);
        int rows=Math.Min(maxRows,(count+columns-1)/columns);
        float ideal=56+columns*510+(columns-1)*14;
        bool showPreview=preview&&ideal+400<=maxWidth;
        float width=Math.Min(maxWidth,Math.Max(900,ideal+(showPreview?400:0)));
        float height=Math.Min(maxHeight,Math.Max(590,300+rows*pitch));
        return new(columns,rows,pitch,width,height,showPreview);
    }
}
~~~~~

### B.09 SettingsForm.cs

<!-- ALTTABFLOW_FILE {"path": "SettingsForm.cs", "encoding": "utf8", "sha256": "6810008431737c6160780879835cc8cd4d7c921e5bc034c1a63571ca9b6bca4e", "bytes": 11131} -->
~~~~~csharp
namespace AltTabFlow;

internal sealed class SettingsForm : Form
{
    private readonly Preferences prefs;
    private readonly CheckBox replace=new(){Text="Alt+Tab을 AltTabFlow로 바꾸기"};
    private readonly CheckBox grouped=new(){Text="같은 앱의 창 묶기 · 제목 함께 표시"};
    private readonly CheckBox preview=new(){Text="목록형에서 선택한 창 미리보기"};
    private readonly CheckBox monitor=new(){Text="현재 작업 중인 모니터의 창만 표시"};
    private readonly CheckBox primary=new(){Text="항상 주 모니터에 전환 화면 표시"};
    private readonly CheckBox startup=new(){Text="Windows 시작 시 자동 실행"};
    private readonly CheckBox highlight=new(){Text="선택한 창 모서리에 순환 아우라 표시"};
    private readonly CheckBox emphasis=new(){Text="Alt+Tab 포커스 강조 · 주변 딤드 + 강한 아우라"};
    private readonly CheckBox peek=new(){Text="선택 중인 대상 창을 앞으로 미리 보여주기"};
    private readonly ComboBox mode=new(){DropDownStyle=ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList};
    private readonly ComboBox size=new(){DropDownStyle=ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList};
    private readonly ComboBox theme=new(){DropDownStyle=ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList};
    private readonly CheckedListBox apps=new(){CheckOnClick=true,IntegralHeight=false,BorderStyle=BorderStyle.FixedSingle};
    private readonly TextBox excluded=new(){Multiline=true,ScrollBars=ScrollBars.Vertical};
    private readonly TextBox manual=new(){PlaceholderText="프로세스 이름 입력 (예: chrome.exe)"};
    private readonly Panel sample=new(){Size=new Size(560,140)};
    private readonly Label registered=new(){AutoSize=true};
    private readonly List<AppOption> choices=new();
    private sealed record AppOption(string Process,string Name){public override string ToString()=>Name.Equals(Process,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)?Name:$"{Name}  ({Process})";}
    public SettingsForm(Preferences prefs,IEnumerable<WindowInfo> windows) {
        this.prefs=prefs;
        Text="AltTabFlow 설정";StartPosition=FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        FormBorderStyle=FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;MaximizeBox=MinimizeBox=false;
        ShowInTaskbar=true;TopMost=true;AutoScaleMode=AutoScaleMode.Dpi;
        AutoScaleDimensions=new SizeF(96,96);ClientSize=new Size(650,660);Font=new Font("맑은 고딕",10);
        var heading=new Label{Text="내 방식으로 창 전환",Font=new Font(Font.FontFamily,18,FontStyle.Bold),Location=new Point(24,18),AutoSize=true};Controls.Add(heading);
        var tabs=new TabControl{Location=new Point(24,72),Size=new Size(602,515)};Controls.Add(tabs);
        TabPage Page(string name){var page=new TabPage(name){Padding=new Padding(12),AutoScroll=true};tabs.TabPages.Add(page);return page;}
        var general=Page("기본 동작");var ordering=Page("상단 정렬");var colors=Page("색상 · 효과");var hiding=Page("숨길 앱");
        Label LabelAt(Control host,string text,int x,int y,int width,int height=28){var label=new Label{Text=text,Location=new Point(x,y),Size=new Size(width,height)};host.Controls.Add(label);return label;}
        void Place(Control host,Control control,int x,int y,int w,int h){control.SetBounds(x,y,w,h);host.Controls.Add(control);}
        mode.Items.AddRange(new object[]{"목록","카드","아이콘"});mode.SelectedItem=prefs.View;
        size.Items.AddRange(new object[]{"85%","100%","115%","130%","140%"});size.SelectedItem=prefs.UiScale+"%";if(size.SelectedIndex<0)size.SelectedIndex=1;
        LabelAt(general,"기본 보기",16,22,90);Place(general,mode,110,18,145,30);
        LabelAt(general,"화면 크기",290,22,90);Place(general,size,385,18,160,30);
        replace.Checked=prefs.ReplaceAltTab;grouped.Checked=prefs.GroupApps;preview.Checked=prefs.ShowPreview;
        monitor.Checked=prefs.CurrentMonitorOnly;primary.Checked=prefs.OnPrimaryMonitor;startup.Checked=prefs.AutoStartByDefault;
        var checks=new[]{replace,grouped,preview,primary,monitor,startup};
        for(int i=0;i<checks.Length;i++)Place(general,checks[i],16,72+i*42,548,32);
        LabelAt(general,"Alt를 누른 채 Tab 반복 → 선택 이동 · Alt를 놓으면 전환\n방향키 / 묶음 진입 / 검색 → Enter 또는 Esc까지 화면 유지\n\n자동 실행은 기본으로 켜져 있으며, 해제한 선택은 유지됩니다.",16,340,550,116);
        LabelAt(ordering,"체크한 앱이 먼저 표시됩니다.",16,16,550);
        LabelAt(ordering,"상단 등록 앱: A~Z → 가나다순\n나머지 앱: A~Z → 가나다순 · 등록한 순서와 무관합니다.",16,48,550,52);
        foreach(var w in windows.GroupBy(x=>x.ProcessName,StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).Select(g=>g.First()))choices.Add(new(w.ProcessName,w.AppName));
        foreach(string process in prefs.Pinned)if(!choices.Any(x=>x.Process.Equals(process,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))choices.Add(new(process,WindowCatalog.AppName(process)));
        Place(ordering,apps,16,110,550,246);apps.AccessibleName="상단 정렬 프로그램 선택";FillChoices(prefs.Pinned);
        Place(ordering,manual,16,372,428,31);
        var add=new Button{Text="등록",FlatStyle=FlatStyle.Flat};Place(ordering,add,454,370,112,33);
        add.Click+=(_,_)=>AddManual();manual.KeyDown+=(_,e)=>{if(e.KeyCode==Keys.Enter){AddManual();e.SuppressKeyPress=true;}};
        registered.Location=new Point(16,416);ordering.Controls.Add(registered);UpdateCount();
        apps.ItemCheck+=(_,e)=>{if(IsHandleCreated)BeginInvoke((Action)UpdateCount);};
        LabelAt(ordering,"키보드: Tab으로 목록 이동 · ↑↓ 선택 · Space로 등록/해제",16,448,554,24);
        LabelAt(colors,"색상 프리셋",16,20,150);Place(colors,theme,185,16,376,32);
        theme.Items.AddRange(ThemePalette.All);theme.SelectedItem=ThemePalette.Resolve(prefs);
        Place(colors,sample,16,70,550,150);sample.Paint+=(_,e)=>PaintSample(e.Graphics);
        theme.SelectedIndexChanged+=(_,_)=>{ApplyColors();sample.Invalidate();};
        peek.Checked=prefs.PeekTarget;Place(colors,peek,16,236,550,32);
        highlight.Checked=prefs.HighlightTarget;Place(colors,highlight,16,276,550,32);
        emphasis.Checked=prefs.EmphasizeTarget;Place(colors,emphasis,16,316,550,32);
        LabelAt(colors,"프리셋의 강조색으로 빛의 꼬리가 빠르게 순환합니다.\n포커스 강조를 켜면 주변은 어둡게, 대상 창은 선명하게 표시합니다.\n최소화된 창은 실제로 전환할 때 복원합니다.\n저장하면 적용됩니다.",16,370,550,96);
        LabelAt(hiding,"숨길 프로그램 · 한 줄에 하나씩 입력",16,20,550);
        LabelAt(hiding,"예: EXCEL, chrome, slack (.exe는 생략 가능)\n다시 보이게 하려면 이름을 지우고 저장하세요.",16,54,550,58);
        excluded.Text=string.Join(Environment.NewLine,prefs.Excluded);Place(hiding,excluded,16,130,550,220);
        LabelAt(hiding,"전환 목록에서 우클릭해 숨길 수도 있습니다.\n프로그램은 종료되지 않으며 AltTabFlow 목록에서만 제외됩니다.",16,374,550,70);
        var save=new Button{Text="저장",Location=new Point(506,605),Size=new Size(120,36),FlatStyle=FlatStyle.Flat};
        var cancel=new Button{Text="취소",DialogResult=DialogResult.Cancel,Location=new Point(385,605),Size=new Size(106,36),FlatStyle=FlatStyle.Flat};
        save.Click+=(_,_)=>Save();Controls.Add(save);Controls.Add(cancel);AcceptButton=save;CancelButton=cancel;ApplyColors();
    }
    private ThemePalette Palette=>(ThemePalette?)theme.SelectedItem??ThemePalette.Resolve(prefs);
    private void ApplyColors(){
        var p=Palette;BackColor=p.Bg;ForeColor=p.Ink;
        void Style(Control host){foreach(Control c in host.Controls){c.ForeColor=p.Ink;c.BackColor=c is TextBox or ListBox or ComboBox or Button?p.Raised:p.Bg;Style(c);}}
        Style(this);sample.BackColor=p.Surface;sample.Invalidate();
    }
    private void PaintSample(Graphics g){
        var p=Palette;g.Clear(p.Surface);float s=DeviceDpi/96f;
        using var accent=new SolidBrush(p.Accent);using var selected=new SolidBrush(p.Selected);
        g.FillRectangle(selected,12*s,54*s,526*s,58*s);g.FillRectangle(accent,12*s,54*s,4*s,58*s);
        TextRenderer.DrawText(g,"AltTabFlow · "+p.Name,Font,new Point((int)(18*s),(int)(15*s)),p.Ink);
        TextRenderer.DrawText(g,"Chrome  ·  전환 대상",Font,new Point((int)(28*s),(int)(67*s)),p.Accent);
        TextRenderer.DrawText(g,"앱 이름과 창 제목을 함께 표시합니다",Font,new Point((int)(28*s),(int)(91*s)),p.Muted);
    }
    private void FillChoices(IEnumerable<string> selected){
        var set=selected.ToHashSet(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);apps.BeginUpdate();apps.Items.Clear();
        foreach(var option in choices.OrderBy(x=>x.Name,AppNameComparer.Instance))apps.Items.Add(option,set.Contains(option.Process));apps.EndUpdate();
    }
    private void AddManual(){
        var names=Preferences.NormalizeApps(manual.Text.Split(new[]{',','\r','\n'},StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries));
        var selected=apps.CheckedItems.Cast<AppOption>().Select(x=>x.Process).Concat(names).ToList();
        foreach(string process in names)if(!choices.Any(x=>x.Process.Equals(process,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))choices.Add(new(process,WindowCatalog.AppName(process)));
        FillChoices(selected);manual.Clear();UpdateCount();
    }
    private void UpdateCount()=>registered.Text=$"상단 등록 {apps.CheckedItems.Count}개 · 실행 중인 앱과 기존 등록 앱을 표시합니다.";
    protected override void OnShown(EventArgs e){base.OnShown(e);if(Owner!=null){Icon=Owner.Icon;var work=Screen.FromControl(Owner).WorkingArea;Location=new Point(Math.Clamp(Owner.Left+(Owner.Width-Width)/2,work.Left,Math.Max(work.Left,work.Right-Width)),Math.Clamp(Owner.Top+(Owner.Height-Height)/2,work.Top,Math.Max(work.Top,work.Bottom-Height)));}}
    private void Save(){
        try{
            Preferences.StartupEnabled=startup.Checked;prefs.StartWithWindows=startup.Checked;
            prefs.View=mode.SelectedItem?.ToString()??"목록";prefs.UiScale=int.Parse((size.SelectedItem?.ToString()??"100%").TrimEnd('%'));
            prefs.ReplaceAltTab=replace.Checked;prefs.GroupApps=grouped.Checked;prefs.ShowPreview=preview.Checked;
            prefs.CurrentMonitorOnly=monitor.Checked;prefs.OnPrimaryMonitor=primary.Checked;
            prefs.ColorPreset=Palette.Id;prefs.LightTheme=Palette.Light;prefs.HighlightTarget=highlight.Checked;
            prefs.EmphasizeTarget=emphasis.Checked;prefs.PeekTarget=peek.Checked;
            prefs.Excluded=Preferences.NormalizeApps(excluded.Text.Split(new[]{'\r','\n',','},StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries));
            prefs.Pinned=apps.CheckedItems.Cast<AppOption>().OrderBy(x=>x.Name,AppNameComparer.Instance).Select(x=>x.Process).ToList();
            DialogResult=DialogResult.OK;Close();
        }catch(Exception ex){MessageBox.Show(this,"설정을 저장하지 못했습니다.\n"+ex.Message,"AltTabFlow",MessageBoxButtons.OK,MessageBoxIcon.Warning);}
    }
}
~~~~~

~~~~~csharp
namespace AltTabFlow;

internal sealed record ThemePalette(string Id,string Name,bool Light,Color Bg,Color Surface,Color Raised,Color Ink,Color Muted,Color Accent,Color Selected)
{
    private static Color Hex(int rgb)=>Color.FromArgb((rgb>>16)&255,(rgb>>8)&255,rgb&255);
    private static ThemePalette Dark(string id,string name,int bg,int surface,int raised,int accent,int selected)=>
        new(id,name,false,Hex(bg),Hex(surface),Hex(raised),Hex(0xEDF3FB),Hex(0xA6B5C9),Hex(accent),Hex(selected));
    public static readonly ThemePalette[] All={
        Dark("mint","민트 다크",0x12161F,0x1C222E,0x242B39,0x57E0C1,0x1D3939),
        Dark("ocean","오션 블루",0x101A2B,0x19283E,0x22344C,0x75B9FF,0x20415E),
        Dark("violet","바이올렛",0x1C172B,0x292238,0x352D46,0xC4A2FF,0x453259),
        Dark("rose","로즈",0x25181F,0x34242F,0x44303D,0xFFA4C0,0x533246),
        Dark("amber","앰버",0x211D16,0x30291F,0x403629,0xF4CD7C,0x514127),
        Dark("nord","노르딕",0x2E3440,0x3B4252,0x434C5E,0x88C0D0,0x3E5668),
        new("light","클라우드 라이트",true,Hex(0xF4F7FB),Color.White,Hex(0xE8EDF5),Hex(0x192434),Hex(0x53647B),Hex(0x006B65),Hex(0xDAF0E9))
    };
    public static ThemePalette Resolve(Preferences p)=>All.FirstOrDefault(x=>x.Id==p.ColorPreset)??All[p.LightTheme?6:0];
    public override string ToString()=>Name;
}
~~~~~

### B.11 TargetPeek.cs

<!-- ALTTABFLOW_FILE {"path": "TargetPeek.cs", "encoding": "utf8", "sha256": "03ea97c572e4e4a04f423958aeafb15fe894f82a6ab9f4be8387fe68ebf9e752", "bytes": 5273} -->
~~~~~csharp
namespace AltTabFlow;

// Raise without taking keyboard focus. Undo temporary Z-order before the next
// preview/cancel/commit, so alphabetical browsing does not alter the MRU history.
internal sealed class TargetPeek : IDisposable
{
    private nint target, previous, chooser;
    private bool wasTopmost;
    private bool requestAccepted;
    private int lastError;
    private readonly List<nint> originallyTopmostOwned=new();
    private const uint PositionFlags=0x4613; // Async, no activation/size/move/owner movement/changing message.
    private static bool Topmost(nint hwnd)=>((long)Native.GetWindowLongPtr(hwnd,-20)&8)!=0;
    public object Statistics=>new{target=target.ToInt64(),chooser=chooser.ToInt64(),requestAccepted,lastError,wasTopmost,temporaryTopmost=target!=0&&Topmost(target),foreground=Native.GetForegroundWindow().ToInt64()};
    public void Select(nint hwnd,nint switcher){
        if(hwnd==target&&switcher==chooser)return;
        Restore();
        if(!Native.IsWindow(hwnd)||!Native.IsWindowVisible(hwnd)||Native.IsIconic(hwnd)||!Native.IsWindow(switcher))return;
        target=hwnd;chooser=switcher;wasTopmost=Topmost(hwnd);
        previous=Native.GetWindow(hwnd,3);
        while(previous!=0){
            Native.GetWindowThreadProcessId(previous,out uint pid);
            if(pid!=Environment.ProcessId&&Topmost(previous)==wasTopmost)break;
            previous=Native.GetWindow(previous,3);
        }
        // Demoting the root also demotes owned palettes. Remember any that were
        // already topmost so cancellation/commit does not change their setting.
        nint root=Native.GetAncestor(target,3);
        Native.EnumWindows((h,_)=>{if(h!=target&&Native.GetAncestor(h,3)==root&&Topmost(h))originallyTopmostOwned.Add(h);return true;},0);
        Native.GetWindowThreadProcessId(target,out uint targetPid);
        Native.AllowSetForegroundWindow(targetPid);
        // HWND_TOP only raises within the normal band. Position immediately
        // behind the topmost chooser instead, above any obscuring topmost app.
        // This may temporarily promote the target; Restore/Accept undo that band.
        requestAccepted=Native.SetWindowPos(target,chooser,0,0,0,0,PositionFlags);
        lastError=requestAccepted?0:System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.GetLastWin32Error();
    }
    public void Restore(){
        if(target==0)return;
        if(Native.IsWindow(target)){
            RestoreBand();
            if(previous!=0&&Native.IsWindow(previous)&&Topmost(previous)==wasTopmost)
                Native.SetWindowPos(target,previous,0,0,0,0,PositionFlags);
        }
        Clear();
    }
    private void RestoreBand(){
        Native.SetWindowPos(target,wasTopmost?-1:-2,0,0,0,0,PositionFlags);
        foreach(nint h in originallyTopmostOwned)if(Native.IsWindow(h))Native.SetWindowPos(h,-1,0,0,0,0,PositionFlags);
    }
    private void Clear(){target=previous=chooser=0;originallyTopmostOwned.Clear();}
    public void Accept(){if(target!=0&&Native.IsWindow(target))RestoreBand();Clear();}
    public void Dispose()=>Restore();
}

internal sealed class TargetDimmer : IDisposable
{
    private readonly List<DimSurface> surfaces=new();
    private Rectangle lastTarget;
    public void Select(nint target,nint switcher){
        if(!Native.IsWindow(target)||Native.IsIconic(target)){Hide();return;}
        if(Native.DwmGetWindowAttributeRect(target,9,out var r,System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.SizeOf<Native.Rect>())!=0&&!Native.GetWindowRect(target,out r)){Hide();return;}
        var rect=Rectangle.FromLTRB(r.Left,r.Top,r.Right,r.Bottom);
        var screens=Screen.AllScreens;
        while(surfaces.Count<screens.Length)surfaces.Add(new DimSurface());
        for(int i=0;i<surfaces.Count;i++){
            if(i>=screens.Length){surfaces[i].Hide();continue;}
            var surface=surfaces[i];var bounds=screens[i].Bounds;
            if(surface.Bounds!=bounds||lastTarget!=rect||!surface.Visible){
                surface.Bounds=bounds;
                var region=new Region(new Rectangle(Point.Empty,bounds.Size));
                var hole=Rectangle.Intersect(bounds,rect);hole.Offset(-bounds.X,-bounds.Y);
                if(hole.Width>0&&hole.Height>0)region.Exclude(hole);
                var old=surface.Region;surface.Region=region;old?.Dispose();
            }
            if(!surface.Visible){surface.Show();Native.SetWindowPos(surface.Handle,switcher,0,0,0,0,0x13);}
        }
        lastTarget=rect;
    }
    public void Hide(){foreach(var surface in surfaces)surface.Hide();}
    public void Dispose(){foreach(var surface in surfaces)surface.Dispose();surfaces.Clear();}
    private sealed class DimSurface : Form
    {
        public DimSurface(){Text="AltTabFlow focus shade";FormBorderStyle=FormBorderStyle.None;ShowInTaskbar=false;StartPosition=FormStartPosition.Manual;AutoScaleMode=AutoScaleMode.None;BackColor=Color.Black;Opacity=.30;}
        protected override bool ShowWithoutActivation=>true;
        protected override CreateParams CreateParams{get{var p=base.CreateParams;p.ExStyle|=0x20|0x80|0x8000000;return p;}}
        protected override void WndProc(ref Message m){if(m.Msg==0x84){m.Result=-1;return;}if(m.Msg==0x21){m.Result=3;return;}base.WndProc(ref m);}
    }
}
~~~~~
